- **mcp-wiki-test-writer**: Write MCP::Wiki tests with Test::More and Path::Tiny tempdirs — TOC parsing, document read/write, tool execution, on_change events, the real Git::Raw history behind an opt-in guard. Tests never write outside a tempdir and never touch a real wiki or the network; the suite stays green with and without Git::Raw. Use for test additions, regression scaffolding, and coverage of new tools. ([Getty/p5-mcp-wiki](https://github.com/Getty/p5-mcp-wiki/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcp-wiki-test-writer.md) · ★1)
- **mcp-wiki-worker**: Default mcp-wiki worker — implement, refactor, debug, and test code in this distribution, the Dockerfile and the entrypoint included. Pre-loaded with MCP::Wiki architecture (15 MCP tools, heading_path section identity, line-based TOC parser, path confinement, optional Git::Raw history) and Getty's Perl conventions. Also the read-only lane for research and audits. Leaves a commit-ready tree; never commits — commits belong to mcp-wiki-release-manager. ([Getty/p5-mcp-wiki](https://github.com/Getty/p5-mcp-wiki/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcp-wiki-worker.md) · ★1)

- **mcp_opera**: 模型上下文协议（Model Context Protocol，MCP） 是由Anthropic公司提出并开源的一项开放标准协议，旨在标准化大型语言模型与外部数据源和工具之间的交互方式。MCP被誉为AI应用的"USB-C接口"，正如USB-C为设备连接外设提供了标准化方式一样，MCP为AI模型连接不同的数据源和工具提供了统一的标准接口。 ([Tencent/AI-Infra-Guard](https://github.com/Tencent/AI-Infra-Guard/blob/HEAD/agent-scan/agent_scan/prompt/system/agents/mcp_opera.md) · ★7.6k · 2 copies)

Use when a function in tools scores below exact and someone needs to know why. Reads and runs the compiler for dumps; never edits the tree. Use when the target is a whole new image rather than a function of the levelBoard. Works in work/, writes recovery notes, never commits. ([Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware](https://github.com/Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcu-board-bootstrap.md))

Triggers: critical analysis, 크리티컬 분석, 동시성 분석, concurrency audit, クリティカル分析, 关键分析, análisis crítico, Konkurrenzanalyse, analisi di concorrenza Do NOT use for: 일반 코드 리뷰 (code-analyzer 사용), MISRA 검증 (safety-auditor 사용), 아키텍처 설계 (fw-architect 사용). - **mcu-scorer**: Measures the levelBoard MCU rebuild against the stock image and reports a scoreboard - per-function positional and LCS scores, whole-image differing bytes, and whether any previously exact function regressed. Use before and after every source change in tools, or to answer "where are we". Never edits source. - **mcu-scribe**: Records what the MCU recovery learned - updates mcu/levelBoard/README.md, PLAN.md, docs/provenance.md and the session memory after a function closes, a hypothesis is ruled out, or a compiler behaviour is established. ([Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware](https://github.com/Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcu-scribe.md))
- **mcu-source-shaper**: Applies a source-spelling hypothesis to the levelBoard rebuild tree - in an rsync experiment copy first, then the reference tree - rebuilds, verifies no other function or byte regressed, and regenerates the FlashForge patch. ([Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware](https://github.com/Klipper4FlashForge/mcu-firmware/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mcu-source-shaper.md))

Four modes: `audit` (first round), `confirm` (re-check named findings on a fix SHA), `challenge` (rule on a finding only the other auditor raised), `plan` (review an ADR or plan; dispatched with `model: "opus"`, ADR-0118, so Fable itself reviews PRs only). - **mdpro-landing-verifier**: Read-only landing check for mdpro-delegator (rule 70). Give it a peer's claimed SHA, the expected parent (the current master HEAD), the paths the peer says it touched, and the lanes to re-run. It checks ancestry, scope and receipt-store integrity at that SHA, re-runs the lanes in its own detached worktree, and returns PASS, ISSUES or BLOCKED keyed to the SHA. Use it before `git merge --ff-only` of a peer commit. Not for fixing findings or landing: the peer fixes, the delegator rules and fast-forwards. Receipt verdicts are advisory under the [2026-11-04 decision](../../docs/agents/decisions/20… ([anzchy/mdpro](https://github.com/anzchy/mdpro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mdpro-landing-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **mdpro-number-registrar**: Read-only lookup of the next free repository-wide ADR number for mdpro-delegator, and of existing D ids (rule 70). Decision-log files and feature-local ids take no number (ADR-0165); a new item is a GitHub issue and takes no D number (docs/agents/decisions/20261102-new-items-are-github-issues.md). It scans every branch, every worktree and the Mac mini remote, and returns the highest number seen and the next free one, with where each high-water mark was found. Use it before the delegator reserves a number.
